ca541a5f77d882351bb2f9d879b77b265dbfe926: $$X_{s,t}:\mathbb{R}^d \to \mathbb{R}^d$$

76ec50ec02280e32a79274f3c59281d05224352e: $$X_{s,t}(x_s)=x_t$$

80b7d27f97078986b90bf9583563d41878920182: $$\partial_{t}X_{s,t}(x)=u_{t}(X_{s,t}(x))$$

300c0ee39253c509f15c2f6b6a3c61c20361a251: $$X_{s,s}(x)=x$$

2c1c99ab5b7fa7ff1d418caf8bf763a2d4abca6d: $$X_{i,t}(X_{s,i}(x))=X_{s,t}(x)$$

de3968d120a606f2e920736fe40ced0b5ab7a057: $$X_{t,s}(X_{s,t}(x))=x$$

e125a9eee9acece32ab37645d5b661b1e6f9000d: $$\frac{d}{ds} X_{s,t}(x_s) = 0$$

b90bb8527bf2c6e6b53e399572ed7bd1dd66558b: $$\partial_s X_{s,t}(x_s) + u_s(x_s) \cdot \nabla X_{s,t}(x_s) = 0$$

0c35ba9afb4892dae25ea768ee115348064398a5: $$\mathcal{L}_{\text{LMD}}(\hat{X}) = \int_{[0,1]^2} w_{s,t} \mathbb{E} \left[ |\partial_t \hat{X}_{s,t}(I_s) - u_t(\hat{X}_{s,t}(I_s))|^2 \right] ds dt$$

77fbd95e6de9aa022974e24b7961abbd114ef08a: $$\mathcal{L}_{\text{EMD}}(\hat{X}) = \int_{[0,1]^2} w_{s,t} \mathbb{E} \left[ |\partial_s \hat{X}_{s,t}(I_s) + u_s(I_s) \cdot \nabla \hat{X}_{s,t}(I_s)|^2 \right] ds dt$$

25a1df3a10789ad2880462bae002c2ac6b40b770: $$\mathcal{L}_{\text{FMM}}(\hat{X}) = \int_{[0,1]^2} w_{s,t} \left( \mathbb{E} \left[ |\partial_t \hat{X}_{s,t}(\hat{X}_{t,s}(I_t)) - \dot{I}_t|^2 \right] + \mathbb{E} \left[ |\hat{X}_{s,t}(\hat{X}_{t,s}(I_t)) - I_t|^2 \right] \right) ds dt$$

67247e57503d441272f18ff42ede9e94ad75ff2e: $$w_{s,t}=w_{t,s}$$

718f5538bedf920a33e543e74e00803e100c32e1: $$X_{s,t}(x)=x+(t-s)v^\theta_{s,t}(x)$$

0be1a4e99d776bcd08b39d7b550d93858dbfd53f: $$\frac{dx_t}{ds}\neq \frac{dx_t}{dt}=u_t(x_t)$$
